#   tanstack-start-void-e2e
import { web } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import type { E2EConfig } from "e2e";

import { e2eBaseURL } from "./tests/support/server.ts";

// The app command inherits only PATH, HOME, and the temp directories; pass on what the stack reads from the shell.
const forwarded = Object.fromEntries(
  ["CI"].flatMap((name) => {
    const value = process.env[name];

    return value === undefined ? [] : [[name, value] as const];
  })
);

export default {
  assertionTimeout: 15_000,
  retries: 0,
  targets: [
    {
      app: {
        command: {
          args: ["tests/support/start.ts"],
          env: { ...forwarded, E2E_TEST_PORT: new URL(e2eBaseURL).port },
          executable: process.execPath,
          log: ".e2e/server.log",
          startupTimeout: 120_000,
        },
        url: e2eBaseURL,
      },
      engine: web(),
    },
  ],
  tests: "tests/e2e/**/*.e2e.ts",
  trace: "retain-on-failure",
} satisfies E2EConfig;

import { web } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import type { E2EConfig } from "e2e";

import { e2eBaseURL } from "./tests/support/server.ts";

// The app command inherits only PATH, HOME, and the temp directories; pass on what the stack reads from the shell.
const forwarded = Object.fromEntries(
  ["CI", "DATABASE_URL"].flatMap((name) => {
    const value = process.env[name];

    return value === undefined ? [] : [[name, value] as const];
  })
);

export default {
  assertionTimeout: 15_000,
  retries: 0,
  targets: [
    {
      app: {
        command: {
          args: ["tests/support/start.ts"],
          env: { ...forwarded, E2E_TEST_PORT: new URL(e2eBaseURL).port },
          executable: process.execPath,
          log: ".e2e/server.log",
          startupTimeout: 120_000,
        },
        url: e2eBaseURL,
      },
      engine: web(),
    },
  ],
  tests: "tests/e2e/**/*.e2e.ts",
  trace: "retain-on-failure",
} satisfies E2EConfig;

# apps/web/tests/e2e/app.e2e.ts · 14 stacks
#   next-docker-e2e
#   next-expo-docker-e2e
#   spa-docker-e2e
#   spa-electron-docker-e2e
#   spa-electron-expo-docker-e2e
#   spa-electron-expo-void-e2e
#   spa-electron-void-e2e
#   spa-expo-docker-e2e
#   spa-expo-void-e2e
#   spa-void-e2e
#   tanstack-start-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-expo-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-expo-void-e2e
#   tanstack-start-void-e2e
import { test } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import type { Browser } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import { expect } from "e2e";

const visit = async (browser: Browser, path: string) => {
  await browser.goto(path, { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
};

// `browser.reload()` takes no `waitUntil`, so a reload is a visit to the current URL.
const reload = async (browser: Browser) => {
  await visit(browser, await browser.url());
};

test("the home page names the stack", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await expect(screen.getByText("on Vite+.", { exact: false })).toBeVisible();
});

test("the dark theme survives a reload", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Toggle theme" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitemradio", { name: "Dark" }).tap();
  const html = browser.locator("html");
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);

  await reload(browser);
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);
});

import { test } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import type { Browser } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import { expect } from "e2e";

const visit = async (browser: Browser, path: string) => {
  await browser.goto(path, { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
};

// `browser.reload()` takes no `waitUntil`, so a reload is a visit to the current URL.
const reload = async (browser: Browser) => {
  await visit(browser, await browser.url());
};

test("the home page reports the API as connected", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await expect(screen.getByText("Connected")).toBeVisible();
});

test("the dark theme survives a reload", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Toggle theme" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitemradio", { name: "Dark" }).tap();
  const html = browser.locator("html");
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);

  await reload(browser);
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);
});

#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { test } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import type { Browser } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import { expect } from "e2e";
import type { Screen } from "e2e";

import { password, signUp, uniqueEmail } from "../support/api.ts";

const visit = async (browser: Browser, path: string) => {
  await browser.goto(path, { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
};

// `browser.reload()` takes no `waitUntil`, so a reload is a visit to the current URL.
const reload = async (browser: Browser) => {
  await visit(browser, await browser.url());
};

const signUpThroughUi = async (browser: Browser, screen: Screen) => {
  await visit(browser, "/login");
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Need an account? Sign up" }).tap();
  await screen.getByLabel("Name").fill("Ada");
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(uniqueEmail());
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill(password);
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign up" }).tap();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/");
};

test("the home page reports the API as connected", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await expect(screen.getByText("Connected")).toBeVisible();
});

test("a new user signs up and is signed in", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);

  await expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ada" })).toBeVisible();
});

test("an existing user signs in", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  const { email } = await signUp();

  await visit(browser, "/login");
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(email);
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill(password);
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).tap();

  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/");
  await expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Test User" })).toBeVisible();
});

test("a wrong password is rejected", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  const { email } = await signUp();

  await visit(browser, "/login");
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(email);
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill("wrong-password");
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).tap();

  await expect(screen.getByText("Invalid email or password")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/login");
});

test("signing out ends the session", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ada" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Sign out" }).tap();
  await expect(screen.getByRole("banner").getByText("Sign in")).toBeVisible();

  await reload(browser);
  await expect(screen.getByRole("banner").getByText("Sign in")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ada" })).toBeHidden();
});

test("the dark theme survives a reload", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Toggle theme" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitemradio", { name: "Dark" }).tap();
  const html = browser.locator("html");
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);

  await reload(browser);
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);
});

import { test } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import type { Browser } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import { expect } from "e2e";
import type { Screen } from "e2e";

import { createTodo, password, signUp, uniqueEmail } from "../support/api.ts";

const visit = async (browser: Browser, path: string) => {
  await browser.goto(path, { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
};

// `browser.reload()` takes no `waitUntil`, so a reload is a visit to the current URL.
const reload = async (browser: Browser) => {
  await visit(browser, await browser.url());
};

const signUpThroughUi = async (browser: Browser, screen: Screen) => {
  await visit(browser, "/login");
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Need an account? Sign up" }).tap();
  await screen.getByLabel("Name").fill("Ada");
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(uniqueEmail());
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill(password);
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign up" }).tap();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/todos");
};

test("the home page reports the API as connected", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await expect(screen.getByText("Connected")).toBeVisible();
});

test("visitors are sent to sign in before seeing todos", async ({
  browser,
}) => {
  await visit(browser, "/todos");

  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/login?redirect=%2Ftodos");
});

test("a new user signs up and manages todos that persist across reloads", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);
  const emptyState = screen.getByText("No todos yet.");
  await expect(emptyState).toBeVisible();

  await screen.getByLabel("New todo").fill("Buy milk");
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add" }).tap();
  const todo = screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Buy milk" });
  await todo.tap();
  await expect(todo).toBeChecked();

  await reload(browser);
  await expect(todo).toBeChecked();

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete Buy milk" }).tap();
  await expect(emptyState).toBeVisible();
});

test("an existing user signs in and returns to the page they asked for", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  const { email, client } = await signUp();
  await createTodo(client, "Existing todo");

  await visit(browser, "/todos");
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(email);
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill(password);
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).tap();

  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/todos");
  await expect(screen.getByText("Existing todo")).toBeVisible();
});

test("a wrong password is rejected", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  const { email } = await signUp();

  await visit(browser, "/login");
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(email);
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill("wrong-password");
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).tap();

  await expect(screen.getByText("Invalid email or password")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/login");
});

test("signing out ends the session", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ada" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Sign out" }).tap();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/");

  await visit(browser, "/todos");
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/login?redirect=%2Ftodos");
});

test("the dark theme survives a reload", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Toggle theme" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitemradio", { name: "Dark" }).tap();
  const html = browser.locator("html");
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);

  await reload(browser);
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);
});

test("login redirects stay on the app origin after URL normalization", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  const { email } = await signUp();
  const redirect = "/\\example.com";
  await visit(browser, `/login?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(redirect)}`);
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(email);
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill(password);
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).tap();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/todos");

  const expectInternalRedirect = async (value: string) => {
    await visit(browser, `/login?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(value)}`);
    await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/todos");
  };

  await expectInternalRedirect("https://example.com");
  await expectInternalRedirect("//example.com");
  await expectInternalRedirect("/\\example.com");
  await expectInternalRedirect("/\t/example.com");
  await expectInternalRedirect("/\r/example.com");
  await expectInternalRedirect("/\n/example.com");
});

import { test } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import type { Browser } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import { expect } from "e2e";

const visit = async (browser: Browser, path: string) => {
  await browser.goto(path, { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
};

// `browser.reload()` takes no `waitUntil`, so a reload is a visit to the current URL.
const reload = async (browser: Browser) => {
  await visit(browser, await browser.url());
};

test("the home page reports the API as connected", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await expect(screen.getByText("Connected")).toBeVisible();
});

test("navigates from home to todos", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Todos" }).tap();

  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/todos");
  await expect(screen.getByLabel("New todo")).toBeVisible();
});

test("creates, completes, persists and deletes a todo", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  const title = `Write e2e test ${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
  await visit(browser, "/todos");

  await screen.getByLabel("New todo").fill(title);
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add" }).tap();
  const todo = screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: title });
  await expect(todo).not.toBeChecked();
  await todo.tap();
  await expect(todo).toBeChecked();

  await reload(browser);
  await expect(todo).toBeChecked();

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: `Delete ${title}` }).tap();
  await expect(todo).toHaveCount(0);
});

test("the dark theme survives a reload", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Toggle theme" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitemradio", { name: "Dark" }).tap();
  const html = browser.locator("html");
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);

  await reload(browser);
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);
});

# apps/web/tests/e2e/app.e2e.ts · 1 stack
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
import { test } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import type { Browser } from "@e2e-dev/web";
import { expect } from "e2e";
import type { Screen } from "e2e";

import { createTodo, password, signUp, uniqueEmail } from "../support/api.ts";

const visit = async (browser: Browser, path: string) => {
  await browser.goto(path, { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
};

// `browser.reload()` takes no `waitUntil`, so a reload is a visit to the current URL.
const reload = async (browser: Browser) => {
  await visit(browser, await browser.url());
};

const signUpThroughUi = async (browser: Browser, screen: Screen) => {
  await visit(browser, "/login");
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Need an account? Sign up" }).tap();
  await screen.getByLabel("Name").fill("Ada");
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(uniqueEmail());
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill(password);
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign up" }).tap();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/todos");
};

test("the home page reports the API as connected", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await expect(screen.getByText("Connected")).toBeVisible();
});

test("visitors are sent to sign in before seeing todos", async ({
  browser,
}) => {
  await visit(browser, "/todos");

  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/login?redirect=%2Ftodos");
});

test("a new user signs up and manages todos that persist across reloads", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);
  const emptyState = screen.getByText("No todos yet.");
  await expect(emptyState).toBeVisible();

  await screen.getByLabel("New todo").fill("Buy milk");
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add" }).tap();
  const todo = screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Buy milk" });
  await todo.tap();
  await expect(todo).toBeChecked();

  await reload(browser);
  await expect(todo).toBeChecked();

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete Buy milk" }).tap();
  await expect(emptyState).toBeVisible();
});

test("an existing user signs in and returns to the page they asked for", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  const { cookie, email } = await signUp();
  await createTodo(cookie, "Existing todo");

  await visit(browser, "/todos");
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(email);
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill(password);
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).tap();

  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/todos");
  await expect(screen.getByText("Existing todo")).toBeVisible();
});

test("a wrong password is rejected", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  const { email } = await signUp();

  await visit(browser, "/login");
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(email);
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill("wrong-password");
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).tap();

  await expect(screen.getByText("Invalid email or password")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/login");
});

test("signing out ends the session", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ada" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Sign out" }).tap();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/");

  await visit(browser, "/todos");
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/login?redirect=%2Ftodos");
});

test("the dark theme survives a reload", async ({ browser, screen }) => {
  await visit(browser, "/");

  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Toggle theme" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitemradio", { name: "Dark" }).tap();
  const html = browser.locator("html");
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);

  await reload(browser);
  await expect(browser).toHaveClass(html, /\bdark\b/u);
});

test("writes to different rows both complete when their requests overlap", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);

  await screen.getByLabel("New todo").fill("First task");
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add" }).tap();
  await expect(
    screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "First task" })
  ).toBeVisible();
  await screen.getByLabel("New todo").fill("Second task");
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add" }).tap();
  await expect(
    screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Second task" })
  ).toBeVisible();

  // Hold the first real request until the second arrives; no timing-dependent sleeps.
  const bothStarted = Promise.withResolvers<void>();
  let writes = 0;
  await browser.route("**/rpc/", async (route) => {
    if (route.request.postData?.includes('"tag":"todos.update"') === true) {
      writes += 1;

      if (writes === 2) {
        bothStarted.resolve();
      }

      await bothStarted.promise;
    }

    await route.continue();
  });

  await screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "First task" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Second task" }).tap();
  await bothStarted.promise;
  await expect(
    screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "First task" })
  ).toBeChecked();
  await expect(
    screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Second task" })
  ).toBeChecked();
  await reload(browser);
  await expect(
    screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "First task" })
  ).toBeChecked();
  await expect(
    screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Second task" })
  ).toBeChecked();
});

test("a failed sign-out keeps the session and allows retry", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);
  // Model an unavailable upstream, without replacing the successful auth path.
  await browser.route("**/api/auth/sign-out", async (route) => {
    await route.fulfill({ status: 503, json: { message: "Unavailable" } });
  });
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ada" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Sign out" }).tap();
  await expect(
    screen.getByText("Could not sign out. Please try again.")
  ).toBeVisible();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/todos");
  await expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ada" })).toBeVisible();

  await browser.unroute("**/api/auth/sign-out");
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ada" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Sign out" }).tap();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/");
});

test("a new session never sees the previous user's cached todos", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);
  await screen.getByLabel("New todo").fill("Private to the first account");
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add" }).tap();
  await expect(screen.getByText("Private to the first account")).toBeVisible();
  await screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Home", exact: true }).tap();
  await expect(screen.getByText("Connected")).toBeVisible();
  await screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ada" }).tap();
  await screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Sign out" }).tap();
  await expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ada" })).not.toBeVisible();
  await expect(screen.getByText("Connected")).toBeVisible();

  await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);
  await expect(screen.getByText("No todos yet.")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(
    screen.getByText("Private to the first account")
  ).not.toBeVisible();
});

test("login redirects stay on the app origin after URL normalization", async ({
  browser,
  screen,
}) => {
  const { email } = await signUp();
  const redirect = "/\\example.com";
  await visit(browser, `/login?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(redirect)}`);
  await screen.getByLabel("Email").fill(email);
  await screen.getByLabel("Password").fill(password);
  await screen.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).tap();
  await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/todos");

  const expectInternalRedirect = async (value: string) => {
    await visit(browser, `/login?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(value)}`);
    await expect(browser).toHaveURL("/todos");
  };

  await expectInternalRedirect("https://example.com");
  await expectInternalRedirect("//example.com");
  await expectInternalRedirect("/\\example.com");
  await expectInternalRedirect("/\t/example.com");
  await expectInternalRedirect("/\r/example.com");
  await expectInternalRedirect("/\n/example.com");
});

for (const { edit, expected } of [
  { edit: "unchanged", expected: "" },
  { edit: "next todo", expected: "Next task" },
  { edit: "same title", expected: "First task" },
]) {
  test(`creating a todo preserves the draft: ${edit}`, async ({
    browser,
    screen,
  }) => {
    await signUpThroughUi(browser, screen);
    const input = screen.getByLabel("New todo");
    const add = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add" });
    const started = Promise.withResolvers<void>();
    const release = Promise.withResolvers<void>();

    await browser.route("**/rpc/", async (route) => {
      if (route.request.postData?.includes('"tag":"todos.create"') === true) {
        started.resolve();
        await release.promise;
      }

      await route.continue();
    });

    try {
      await input.fill("First task");
      await add.tap();
      await started.promise;

      if (edit !== "unchanged") {
        await input.fill("Next task");
      }

      if (edit === "same title") {
        await input.fill("First task");
      }
    } finally {
      release.resolve();
    }

    await expect(
      screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "First task" })
    ).toBeVisible();
    await expect(input).toHaveValue(expected);

    if (edit !== "unchanged") {
      await add.tap();
      await expect(screen.getByRole("checkbox")).toHaveCount(2);
      await expect(input).toHaveValue("");
    }
  });
}

import { e2eBaseURL } from "./server.ts";

export const password = "correct-horse-battery";

export const uniqueEmail = () => `${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.com`;

/** Signs up through the running server, as the sign-up form does. */
export const signUp = async (email = uniqueEmail()) => {
  const response = await fetch(`${e2eBaseURL}/api/auth/sign-up/email`, {
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, name: "Test User", password }),
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", origin: e2eBaseURL },
    method: "POST",
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Sign up failed with ${response.status}`);
  }

  return { email };
};

#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-mysql-prisma-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-postgres-better-auth-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-postgres-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-postgres-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-postgres-prisma-better-auth-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-postgres-prisma-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-postgres-prisma-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-postgres-prisma-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-sqlite-better-auth-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-sqlite-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-sqlite-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { hc, parseResponse } from "hono/client";

import type { Api } from "@my-app/api";

import { e2eBaseURL } from "./server.ts";

type Client = ReturnType<typeof hc<Api>>;

export const password = "correct-horse-battery";

export const uniqueEmail = () => `${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.com`;

export const createTodo = async (client: Client, title: string) =>
  await parseResponse(client.todos.$post({ json: { title } }));

/** Signs up through the running server, as the sign-up form does, and returns a client that calls as the new user. */
export const signUp = async (email = uniqueEmail()) => {
  const response = await fetch(`${e2eBaseURL}/api/auth/sign-up/email`, {
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, name: "Test User", password }),
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", origin: e2eBaseURL },
    method: "POST",
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Sign up failed with ${response.status}`);
  }

  const cookie = response.headers
    .getSetCookie()
    .map((header) => header.split(";")[0])
    .join("; ");

  return {
    client: hc<Api>(`${e2eBaseURL}/api`, { headers: { cookie } }),
    email,
  };
};

#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { createORPCClient } from "@orpc/client";
import { RPCLink } from "@orpc/client/fetch";

import type { AppRouterClient } from "@my-app/api";

import { e2eBaseURL } from "./server.ts";

export const password = "correct-horse-battery";

export const uniqueEmail = () => `${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.com`;

export const createTodo = async (client: AppRouterClient, title: string) =>
  await client.todos.create({ title });

/** Signs up through the running server, as the sign-up form does, and returns a client that calls as the new user. */
export const signUp = async (email = uniqueEmail()) => {
  const response = await fetch(`${e2eBaseURL}/api/auth/sign-up/email`, {
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, name: "Test User", password }),
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", origin: e2eBaseURL },
    method: "POST",
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Sign up failed with ${response.status}`);
  }

  const cookie = response.headers
    .getSetCookie()
    .map((header) => header.split(";")[0])
    .join("; ");

  const client: AppRouterClient = createORPCClient(
    new RPCLink({ headers: { cookie }, url: `${e2eBaseURL}/rpc` })
  );

  return { client, email };
};

# apps/web/tests/support/api.ts · 1 stack
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
import { Effect, Layer } from "effect";
import { FetchHttpClient } from "effect/http";
import { RpcClient, RpcSerialization } from "effect/rpc";

import { TodosRpcs } from "@my-app/contracts";

import { e2eBaseURL } from "./server.ts";

export const password = "correct-horse-battery";

export const uniqueEmail = () => `${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.com`;

export const createTodo = async (cookie: string, title: string) => {
  const program = Effect.gen(function* () {
    const client = yield* RpcClient.make(TodosRpcs, { flatten: true });

    return yield* client("todos.create", { title });
  }).pipe(
    Effect.scoped,
    Effect.provide(
      RpcClient.layerProtocolHttp({ url: `${e2eBaseURL}/rpc` }).pipe(
        Layer.provide([RpcSerialization.layerNdjson, FetchHttpClient.layer]),
        Layer.provide(
          Layer.succeed(FetchHttpClient.Fetch, async (input, init) => {
            const request = new Request(input, init);
            request.headers.set("cookie", cookie);

            return await fetch(request);
          })
        )
      )
    )
  );

  return await Effect.runPromise(program);
};

export const signUp = async (email = uniqueEmail()) => {
  const response = await fetch(`${e2eBaseURL}/api/auth/sign-up/email`, {
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, name: "Test User", password }),
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", origin: e2eBaseURL },
    method: "POST",
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Sign up failed with ${response.status}`);
  }

  const cookie = response.headers
    .getSetCookie()
    .map((header) => header.split(";")[0])
    .join("; ");

  return { cookie, email };
};

import { RPCLink } from "@orpc/client/fetch";

import type { AppRouterClient } from "@my-app/api";

import { e2eBaseURL } from "./server.ts";

export const password = "correct-horse-battery";

export const uniqueEmail = () => `${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.com`;

export const createTodo = async (client: AppRouterClient, title: string) =>
  await client.todos.create({ title });

/** Signs up through the running server, as the sign-up form does, and returns a client that calls as the new user. */
export const signUp = async (email = uniqueEmail()) => {

    body: JSON.stringify({ email, name: "Test User", password }),

  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Sign up failed with ${response.status}`);

import { Agent, setGlobalDispatcher } from "undici";

// Node 24's bundled Undici can crash on macOS socket QoS calls (nodejs/undici#5544), so tests use the catalog's fixed dispatcher.
const dispatcher = new Agent();

setGlobalDispatcher(dispatcher);

export const closeTestHttp = async () => {
  await dispatcher.close();
};

# apps/web/tests/support/server.ts · 2 stacks
#   next-docker-e2e
#   next-expo-docker-e2e
import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 300): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (cwd: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>) =>
  spawn(process.execPath, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  const { port } = new URL(baseURL);
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        ["node_modules/next/dist/bin/next", "dev", "--port", port],
        { NEXT_DIST_DIR: `.next-test-${port}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/`);
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 300): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (
  cwd: string,
  args: string[],
  env: Record<string, string>,
  executable = process.execPath
) =>
  spawn(executable, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(...(await Promise.allSettled([closeTestHttp()])));

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(serverDir, ["src/index.ts"], { PORT: serverPort }, "bun")
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        ["node_modules/next/dist/bin/next", "dev", "--port", String(webPort)],
        {
          NEXT_DIST_DIR: `.next-test-${webPort}`,
          SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}`,
        }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 300): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

  cwd: string,
  args: string[],
  env: Record<string, string>,
  executable = process.execPath
) =>
  spawn(executable, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {

          }

        }

      }),
    ]);
  };

  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(
      ...(await Promise.allSettled([database.remove(), closeTestHttp()]))
    );

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(
        serverDir,
        ["src/index.ts"],
        {
          BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: "test-secret-that-is-at-least-32-characters",
          BETTER_AUTH_URL: baseURL,
          DATABASE_URL: database.url,
          PORT: serverPort,
        },
        "bun"
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        ["node_modules/next/dist/bin/next", "dev", "--port", String(webPort)],
        {
          NEXT_DIST_DIR: `.next-test-${webPort}`,
          SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}`,
        }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/", "/login"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

        ["node_modules/next/dist/bin/next", "dev", "--port", String(webPort)],
        {
          NEXT_DIST_DIR: `.next-test-${webPort}`,
          SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}`,
        }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/", "/login"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 300): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (
  cwd: string,
  args: string[],
  env: Record<string, string>,
  executable = process.execPath
) =>
  spawn(executable, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(
      ...(await Promise.allSettled([database.remove(), closeTestHttp()]))
    );

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(
        serverDir,
        ["src/index.ts"],
        { DATABASE_URL: database.url, PORT: serverPort },
        "bun"
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        ["node_modules/next/dist/bin/next", "dev", "--port", String(webPort)],
        {
          NEXT_DIST_DIR: `.next-test-${webPort}`,
          SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}`,
        }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 300): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (
  cwd: string,
  args: string[],
  env: Record<string, string>,
  executable = process.execPath
) =>
  spawn(executable, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(
      ...(await Promise.allSettled([database.remove(), closeTestHttp()]))
    );

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(
        serverDir,
        ["src/index.ts"],
        { DATABASE_URL: database.url, PORT: serverPort },
        "bun"
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        ["node_modules/next/dist/bin/next", "dev", "--port", String(webPort)],
        {
          NEXT_DIST_DIR: `.next-test-${webPort}`,
          SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}`,
        }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/", "/todos"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${webDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 300): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (cwd: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>) =>
  spawn(process.execPath, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const { port } = new URL(baseURL);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(
      ...(await Promise.allSettled([database.remove(), closeTestHttp()]))
    );

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        ["node_modules/next/dist/bin/next", "dev", "--port", port],
        {
          BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: "test-secret-that-is-at-least-32-characters",
          BETTER_AUTH_URL: baseURL,
          DATABASE_URL: database.url,
          NEXT_DIST_DIR: `.next-test-${port}`,
        }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/", "/login", "/todos"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { Effect, Redacted, Schedule } from "effect";
import { ChildProcess } from "effect/process";

import { makeTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = Effect.fn("TestServer.waitForServer")(function* (
  url: string
) {
  yield* Effect.tryPromise(async (signal) => {
    const response = await fetch(url, { signal });
    await response.arrayBuffer();

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
    }
  }).pipe(
    Effect.retry(Schedule.spaced("100 millis")),
    Effect.timeout("60 seconds")
  );
});

const start = (cwd: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>) =>
  ChildProcess.make(process.execPath, args, {
    cwd,
    env,
    extendEnv: true,
    forceKillAfter: "5 seconds",
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI.
    stdin: "pipe",
    stdout: "inherit",
    stderr: "inherit",
  });

export const testServer = Effect.gen(function* () {
  yield* Effect.sync(() => {
    if (existsSync(envFile)) {
      process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
    }
  });

  const webPort = Number(new URL(e2eBaseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const databaseUrl = yield* makeTestDatabase;

  yield* start(serverDir, ["src/index.ts"], {
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: "test-secret-that-is-at-least-32-characters",
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: e2eBaseURL,
    DATABASE_URL: Redacted.value(databaseUrl),
    PORT: serverPort,
  });
  yield* waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
  yield* start(
    webDir,
    [
      "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
      "dev",
      "--port",
      String(webPort),
      "--strictPort",
    ],
    {
      SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}`,
    }
  );
  yield* waitForServer(`${e2eBaseURL}/api/health`);
  yield* Effect.forEach(
    ["/", "/login", "/todos"],
    (route) => waitForServer(`${e2eBaseURL}${route}`),
    { concurrency: "unbounded" }
  );
  yield* Effect.never;
});

  return stopAll;
};

import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 100): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (cwd: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>) =>
  spawn(process.execPath, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(...(await Promise.allSettled([closeTestHttp()])));

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(start(serverDir, ["src/index.ts"], { PORT: serverPort }));
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 100): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (
  cwd: string,
  args: string[],
  env: Record<string, string>,
  executable = process.execPath
) =>
  spawn(executable, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(
      ...(await Promise.allSettled([database.remove(), closeTestHttp()]))
    );

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(
        serverDir,
        ["src/index.ts"],
        { DATABASE_URL: database.url, PORT: serverPort },
        "bun"
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    }
  };

    children.push(
      start(
        serverDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          serverPort,
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        {
          VOID_INSPECTOR_PORT: "false",
          BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: "test-secret-that-is-at-least-32-characters",
          BETTER_AUTH_URL: baseURL,
          DATABASE_URL: database.url,
        }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/", "/login"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

};

import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

#   spa-hono-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e
import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 100): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (
  cwd: string,
  args: string[],
  env: Record<string, string>,
  executable = process.execPath
) =>
  spawn(executable, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(
      ...(await Promise.allSettled([database.remove(), closeTestHttp()]))
    );

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(
        serverDir,
        ["src/index.ts"],
        {
          BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: "test-secret-that-is-at-least-32-characters",
          BETTER_AUTH_URL: baseURL,
          DATABASE_URL: database.url,
          PORT: serverPort,
        },
        "bun"
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/", "/login", "/todos"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

#   spa-hono-orpc-sqlite-electron-expo-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-orpc-sqlite-expo-docker-e2e
import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 100): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (cwd: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>) =>
  spawn(process.execPath, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(
      ...(await Promise.allSettled([database.remove(), closeTestHttp()]))
    );

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(serverDir, ["src/index.ts"], {
        DATABASE_URL: database.url,
        PORT: serverPort,
      })
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/", "/todos"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { once } from "node:events";

import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],

    child.signalCode !== null

  }

  const { pid } = child;

              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

      }),
    ]);
  };

  } catch (error) {

  }

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);

    results.push(...(await Promise.allSettled([closeTestHttp()])));

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

    children.push(
      start(serverDir, ["src/index.ts"], { PORT: serverPort }, "bun")
    );

    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { PORT: String(webPort), SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};
