#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-expo-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-orpc-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-orpc-expo-docker-e2e
import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 100): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (cwd: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>) =>
  spawn(process.execPath, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(...(await Promise.allSettled([closeTestHttp()])));

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(start(serverDir, ["src/index.ts"], { PORT: serverPort }));
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { PORT: String(webPort), SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

const start = (
  cwd: string,
  args: string[],
  env: Record<string, string>,
  executable = process.execPath
) =>

    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },

    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],

  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(
      ...(await Promise.allSettled([database.remove(), closeTestHttp()]))
    );

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(
        serverDir,
        ["src/index.ts"],
        {
          BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: "test-secret-that-is-at-least-32-characters",
          BETTER_AUTH_URL: baseURL,
          DATABASE_URL: database.url,
          PORT: serverPort,
        },
        "bun"
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { PORT: String(webPort), SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/", "/login"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(serverDir, ["src/index.ts"], {
        DATABASE_URL: database.url,
        PORT: serverPort,
      })
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { PORT: String(webPort), SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const serverDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../server", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${serverDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 100): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (
  cwd: string,
  args: string[],
  env: Record<string, string>,
  executable = process.execPath
) =>
  spawn(executable, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(
      ...(await Promise.allSettled([database.remove(), closeTestHttp()]))
    );

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(
        serverDir,
        ["src/index.ts"],
        {
          BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: "test-secret-that-is-at-least-32-characters",
          BETTER_AUTH_URL: baseURL,
          DATABASE_URL: database.url,
          PORT: serverPort,
        },
        "bun"
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { PORT: String(webPort), SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/", "/login", "/todos"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const webPort = Number(new URL(baseURL).port);
  const serverPort = String(webPort + 1);
  const database = await createTestDatabase();
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(
      ...(await Promise.allSettled([database.remove(), closeTestHttp()]))
    );

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(serverDir, ["src/index.ts"], {
        DATABASE_URL: database.url,
        PORT: serverPort,
      })
    );
    await waitForServer(`http://localhost:${serverPort}/api/health`);
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          String(webPort),
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { PORT: String(webPort), SERVER_URL: `http://localhost:${serverPort}` }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/", "/todos"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${webDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 100): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (cwd: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>) =>
  spawn(process.execPath, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;

import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import type { ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { retry } from "es-toolkit/function";

import { closeTestHttp } from "./http.ts";

export const e2eBaseURL = `http://localhost:${process.env.E2E_TEST_PORT ?? "3200"}`;

const webDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url));
const envFile = `${webDir}/.env`;

const waitForServer = async (url: string, attempts = 300): Promise<void> => {
  await retry(
    async () => {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      await response.arrayBuffer();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Server did not become healthy at ${url}`);
      }
    },
    { delay: 100, retries: attempts }
  );
};

const start = (cwd: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>) =>
  spawn(process.execPath, args, {
    cwd,
    detached: true,
    env: { ...process.env, ...env },
    // Vite closes its server on stdin EOF outside CI; keep this pipe open until teardown.
    stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });

const stop = async (child: ChildProcess) => {
  if (
    child.pid === undefined ||
    child.exitCode !== null ||
    child.signalCode !== null
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const { pid } = child;

  const terminate = async (signal: "SIGTERM" | "SIGKILL") => {
    await Promise.all([
      once(child, "exit", { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) }),
      Promise.resolve().then(() => {
        if (process.platform === "win32") {
          // taskkill may report already-exited descendants as errors.
          const result = spawnSync(
            "taskkill",
            ["/pid", String(pid), "/t", "/f"],
            {
              stdio: "ignore",
              timeout: 5000,
            }
          );

          if (result.error || result.status !== 0) {
            return child.kill();
          }

          return true;
        }

        return process.kill(-pid, signal);
      }),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    await terminate("SIGTERM");
  } catch (error) {
    if (process.platform === "win32") {
      throw error;
    }

    await terminate("SIGKILL");
  }
};

export const startTestServer = async (baseURL: string) => {
  if (existsSync(envFile)) {
    process.loadEnvFile(envFile);
  }

  const { port } = new URL(baseURL);
  const children: ChildProcess[] = [];

  const stopAll = async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(children.map(stop));

    results.push(...(await Promise.allSettled([closeTestHttp()])));

    const errors: unknown[] = [];

    for (const result of results) {
      if (result.status === "rejected") {
        errors.push(result.reason);
      }
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      throw new AggregateError(errors, "Failed to stop test servers");
    }
  };

  try {
    children.push(
      start(
        webDir,
        [
          "node_modules/vite-plus/bin/vp",
          "dev",
          "--port",
          port,
          "--strictPort",
        ],
        { VOID_INSPECTOR_PORT: "false" }
      )
    );
    await waitForServer(`${baseURL}/api/health`);
    await Promise.all(
      ["/"].map(async (route) => {
        const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}${route}`);
        await response.arrayBuffer();
      })
    );
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      await stopAll();
    } catch (cleanupError) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [error, cleanupError],
        "Test server startup and cleanup failed",
        { cause: cleanupError }
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  return stopAll;
};

import { once } from "node:events";

import { e2eBaseURL, startTestServer } from "./server.ts";

const stopped = Promise.race([
  once(process, "SIGINT"),
  once(process, "SIGTERM"),
]);

const stop = await startTestServer(e2eBaseURL);

try {
  await stopped;
} finally {
  await stop();
}

  testServer.pipe(Effect.scoped, Effect.provide(NodeServices.layer))
);

# packages/api/tests/integration/todos.test.ts · 16 stacks
#   next-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-bun-docker-e2e
#   next-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   next-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e
#   next-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-bun-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-electron-bun-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-electron-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-electron-expo-bun-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-electron-expo-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-mongodb-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { parseResponse } from "hono/client";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vite-plus/test";

import { api, createClient, signUp } from "../support/api.ts";

type Client = ReturnType<typeof createClient>;

const anonymous = createClient();
const param = { id: "1" };

const createTodo = async (client: Client, title: string) => {
  const response = await client.todos.$post({ json: { title } });
  expect(response.status).toBe(201);

  return await parseResponse(response);
};

describe("todos", () => {
  it.each([
    ["GET /todos", async () => await anonymous.todos.$get()],
    [
      "POST /todos",
      async () => await anonymous.todos.$post({ json: { title: "Buy milk" } }),
    ],
    [
      "PATCH /todos/:id",
      async () =>
        await anonymous.todos[":id"].$patch({
          json: { completed: true },
          param,
        }),
    ],
    [
      "DELETE /todos/:id",
      async () => await anonymous.todos[":id"].$delete({ param }),
    ],
  ])("rejects an anonymous %s with 401", async (_, call) => {
    await expect(call()).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 401);
  });

  it("creates a todo with a trimmed title, not yet completed", async () => {
    const client = await signUp();

    const todo = await createTodo(client, "  Buy milk  ");

    expect(todo).toMatchObject({ completed: false, title: "Buy milk" });
    await expect(parseResponse(client.todos.$get())).resolves.toStrictEqual([
      todo,
    ]);
  });

  it.each(["", "   ", "x".repeat(201)])(
    "rejects the title %j with 400",
    async (title) => {
      const client = await signUp();

      await expect(
        client.todos.$post({ json: { title } })
      ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 400);
    }
  );

  it("completes a todo, then deletes it once", async () => {
    const client = await signUp();
    const todo = await createTodo(client, "Buy milk");
    const own = { id: todo.id };

    await expect(
      parseResponse(
        client.todos[":id"].$patch({ json: { completed: true }, param: own })
      )
    ).resolves.toStrictEqual({ ...todo, completed: true });
    await expect(
      client.todos[":id"].$delete({ param: own })
    ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 200);
    await expect(
      client.todos[":id"].$delete({ param: own })
    ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 404);
  });

  it("keeps each user's todos private", async () => {
    const [alice, bob] = await Promise.all([signUp(), signUp()]);
    const todo = await createTodo(alice, "Alice's secret");
    const own = { id: todo.id };

    await expect(parseResponse(bob.todos.$get())).resolves.toStrictEqual([]);
    await expect(
      bob.todos[":id"].$patch({ json: { completed: true }, param: own })
    ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 404);
    await expect(
      bob.todos[":id"].$delete({ param: own })
    ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 404);
    await expect(parseResponse(alice.todos.$get())).resolves.toStrictEqual([
      todo,
    ]);
  });

  it("describes every route in the OpenAPI document", async () => {
    const response = await api.request("/openapi.json");

    await expect(response.json()).resolves.toMatchObject({
      paths: {
        "/health": { get: {} },
        "/todos": { get: {}, post: {} },
        "/todos/{id}": { delete: {}, patch: {} },
      },
      servers: [{ url: "/api" }],
    });
  });
});

#   spa-hono-openapi-mongodb-electron-expo-bun-docker-e2e

#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-mongodb-expo-docker-e2e
import { parseResponse } from "hono/client";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vite-plus/test";

import { api, client } from "../support/api.ts";

const createTodo = async (title: string) => {
  const response = await client.todos.$post({ json: { title } });
  expect(response.status).toBe(201);

  return await parseResponse(response);
};

describe("todos", () => {
  it("creates a todo with a trimmed title, not yet completed", async () => {
    const todo = await createTodo("  Buy milk  ");

    expect(todo).toMatchObject({ completed: false, title: "Buy milk" });
    await expect(parseResponse(client.todos.$get())).resolves.toContainEqual(
      todo
    );
  });

  it.each(["", "   ", "x".repeat(201)])(
    "rejects the title %j with 400",
    async (title) => {
      await expect(
        client.todos.$post({ json: { title } })
      ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 400);
    }
  );

  it("completes a todo, then deletes it once", async () => {
    const todo = await createTodo("Buy milk");
    const param = { id: todo.id };

    await expect(
      parseResponse(
        client.todos[":id"].$patch({ json: { completed: true }, param })
      )
    ).resolves.toStrictEqual({ ...todo, completed: true });
    await expect(
      client.todos[":id"].$delete({ param })
    ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 200);
    await expect(
      client.todos[":id"].$delete({ param })
    ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 404);
  });

  it("describes every route in the OpenAPI document", async () => {
    const response = await api.request("/openapi.json");

    await expect(response.json()).resolves.toMatchObject({
      paths: {
        "/health": { get: {} },
        "/todos": { get: {}, post: {} },
        "/todos/{id}": { delete: {}, patch: {} },
      },
      servers: [{ url: "/api" }],
    });
  });
});

#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-postgres-prisma-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-postgres-prisma-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e

#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-sqlite-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { parseResponse } from "hono/client";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vite-plus/test";

import { api, createClient, signUp } from "../support/api.ts";

type Client = ReturnType<typeof createClient>;

const anonymous = createClient();
const param = { id: "1" };

const createTodo = async (client: Client, title: string) => {
  const response = await client.todos.$post({ json: { title } });
  expect(response.status).toBe(201);

  return await parseResponse(response);
};

describe("todos", () => {
  it.each([
    ["GET /todos", async () => await anonymous.todos.$get()],
    [
      "POST /todos",
      async () => await anonymous.todos.$post({ json: { title: "Buy milk" } }),
    ],
    [
      "PATCH /todos/:id",
      async () =>
        await anonymous.todos[":id"].$patch({
          json: { completed: true },
          param,
        }),
    ],
    [
      "DELETE /todos/:id",
      async () => await anonymous.todos[":id"].$delete({ param }),
    ],
  ])("rejects an anonymous %s with 401", async (_, call) => {
    await expect(call()).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 401);
  });

  it("creates a todo with a trimmed title, not yet completed", async () => {
    const client = await signUp();

    const todo = await createTodo(client, "  Buy milk  ");

    expect(todo).toMatchObject({ completed: false, title: "Buy milk" });
    await expect(parseResponse(client.todos.$get())).resolves.toStrictEqual([
      todo,
    ]);
  });

  it.each(["", "   ", "x".repeat(201)])(
    "rejects the title %j with 400",
    async (title) => {
      const client = await signUp();

      await expect(
        client.todos.$post({ json: { title } })
      ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 400);
    }
  );

  it("completes a todo, then deletes it once", async () => {
    const client = await signUp();
    const todo = await createTodo(client, "Buy milk");
    const own = { id: String(todo.id) };

    await expect(
      parseResponse(
        client.todos[":id"].$patch({ json: { completed: true }, param: own })
      )
    ).resolves.toStrictEqual({ ...todo, completed: true });
    await expect(
      client.todos[":id"].$delete({ param: own })
    ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 200);
    await expect(
      client.todos[":id"].$delete({ param: own })
    ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 404);
  });

  it("keeps each user's todos private", async () => {
    const [alice, bob] = await Promise.all([signUp(), signUp()]);
    const todo = await createTodo(alice, "Alice's secret");
    const own = { id: String(todo.id) };

    await expect(parseResponse(bob.todos.$get())).resolves.toStrictEqual([]);
    await expect(
      bob.todos[":id"].$patch({ json: { completed: true }, param: own })
    ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 404);
    await expect(
      bob.todos[":id"].$delete({ param: own })
    ).resolves.toHaveProperty("status", 404);
    await expect(parseResponse(alice.todos.$get())).resolves.toStrictEqual([
      todo,
    ]);
  });

  it("describes every route in the OpenAPI document", async () => {
    const response = await api.request("/openapi.json");

    await expect(response.json()).resolves.toMatchObject({
      paths: {
        "/health": { get: {} },
        "/todos": { get: {}, post: {} },
        "/todos/{id}": { delete: {}, patch: {} },
      },
      servers: [{ url: "/api" }],
    });
  });
});

#   tanstack-start-hono-orpc-mongodb-better-auth-expo-bun-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-hono-orpc-mongodb-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mongodb-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mongodb-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { describe, expect, it } from "vite-plus/test";

import { createClient, signUp } from "../support/api.ts";

const anonymous = createClient();

describe("todos", () => {
  it.each([
    ["list", async () => await anonymous.todos.list()],
    ["create", async () => await anonymous.todos.create({ title: "Buy milk" })],
    [
      "update",
      async () =>
        await anonymous.todos.update({
          completed: true,
          id: "0123456789abcdef01234567",
        }),
    ],
    [
      "delete",
      async () =>
        await anonymous.todos.delete({ id: "0123456789abcdef01234567" }),
    ],
  ])("rejects an anonymous %s with UNAUTHORIZED", async (_, call) => {
    await expect(call()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "UNAUTHORIZED" });
  });

  it("creates a todo with a trimmed title, not yet completed", async () => {
    const client = await signUp();

    const todo = await client.todos.create({ title: "  Buy milk  " });

    expect(todo).toMatchObject({ completed: false, title: "Buy milk" });
    await expect(client.todos.list()).resolves.toStrictEqual([todo]);
  });

  it.each(["", "   ", "x".repeat(201)])(
    "rejects the title %j with BAD_REQUEST",
    async (title) => {
      const client = await signUp();

      await expect(client.todos.create({ title })).rejects.toMatchObject({
        code: "BAD_REQUEST",
      });
    }
  );

  it("completes a todo, then deletes it once", async () => {
    const client = await signUp();
    const todo = await client.todos.create({ title: "Buy milk" });

    await expect(
      client.todos.update({ completed: true, id: todo.id })
    ).resolves.toStrictEqual({ ...todo, completed: true });
    await client.todos.delete({ id: todo.id });
    await expect(client.todos.list()).resolves.toStrictEqual([]);
    await expect(client.todos.delete({ id: todo.id })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
    });
  });

  it("keeps each user's todos private", async () => {
    const [alice, bob] = await Promise.all([signUp(), signUp()]);
    const todo = await alice.todos.create({ title: "Alice's secret" });

    await expect(bob.todos.list()).resolves.toStrictEqual([]);
    await expect(
      bob.todos.update({ completed: true, id: todo.id })
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "NOT_FOUND" });
    await expect(bob.todos.delete({ id: todo.id })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
    });
    await expect(alice.todos.list()).resolves.toStrictEqual([todo]);
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from "vite-plus/test";

import { client } from "../support/api.ts";

describe("todos", () => {
  it("creates a todo with a trimmed title, not yet completed", async () => {
    const todo = await client.todos.create({ title: "  Buy milk  " });

    expect(todo).toMatchObject({ completed: false, title: "Buy milk" });
    await expect(client.todos.list()).resolves.toContainEqual(todo);
  });

  it.each(["", "   ", "x".repeat(201)])(
    "rejects the title %j with BAD_REQUEST",
    async (title) => {
      await expect(client.todos.create({ title })).rejects.toMatchObject({
        code: "BAD_REQUEST",
      });
    }
  );

  it("completes a todo, then deletes it once", async () => {
    const todo = await client.todos.create({ title: "Buy milk" });

    await expect(
      client.todos.update({ completed: true, id: todo.id })
    ).resolves.toStrictEqual({ ...todo, completed: true });
    await client.todos.delete({ id: todo.id });
    await expect(client.todos.list()).resolves.not.toContainEqual(
      expect.objectContaining({ id: todo.id })
    );
    await expect(client.todos.delete({ id: todo.id })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
    });
  });
});

#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { describe, expect, it } from "vite-plus/test";

import { createClient, signUp } from "../support/api.ts";

const anonymous = createClient();

describe("todos", () => {
  it.each([
    ["list", async () => await anonymous.todos.list()],
    ["create", async () => await anonymous.todos.create({ title: "Buy milk" })],
    [
      "update",
      async () => await anonymous.todos.update({ completed: true, id: 1 }),
    ],
    ["delete", async () => await anonymous.todos.delete({ id: 1 })],
  ])("rejects an anonymous %s with UNAUTHORIZED", async (_, call) => {
    await expect(call()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "UNAUTHORIZED" });
  });

  it("creates a todo with a trimmed title, not yet completed", async () => {
    const client = await signUp();

    const todo = await client.todos.create({ title: "  Buy milk  " });

    expect(todo).toMatchObject({ completed: false, title: "Buy milk" });
    await expect(client.todos.list()).resolves.toStrictEqual([todo]);
  });

  it.each(["", "   ", "x".repeat(201)])(
    "rejects the title %j with BAD_REQUEST",
    async (title) => {
      const client = await signUp();

      await expect(client.todos.create({ title })).rejects.toMatchObject({
        code: "BAD_REQUEST",
      });
    }
  );

  it("completes a todo, then deletes it once", async () => {
    const client = await signUp();
    const todo = await client.todos.create({ title: "Buy milk" });

    await expect(
      client.todos.update({ completed: true, id: todo.id })
    ).resolves.toStrictEqual({ ...todo, completed: true });
    await client.todos.delete({ id: todo.id });
    await expect(client.todos.list()).resolves.toStrictEqual([]);
    await expect(client.todos.delete({ id: todo.id })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
    });
  });

  it("keeps each user's todos private", async () => {
    const [alice, bob] = await Promise.all([signUp(), signUp()]);
    const todo = await alice.todos.create({ title: "Alice's secret" });

    await expect(bob.todos.list()).resolves.toStrictEqual([]);
    await expect(
      bob.todos.update({ completed: true, id: todo.id })
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "NOT_FOUND" });
    await expect(bob.todos.delete({ id: todo.id })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
    });
    await expect(alice.todos.list()).resolves.toStrictEqual([todo]);
  });
});

# packages/api/tests/integration/todos.test.ts · 1 stack
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
import { assert, layer } from "@effect/vitest";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { Effect } from "effect";

import {
  ServiceUnavailable,
  TodoNotFound,
  Unauthorized,
} from "@my-app/contracts";

import { createRaw, makeClient, rpcRaw, TestApp } from "../support/app.ts";

type Client = Effect.Success<ReturnType<typeof makeClient>>;

layer(TestApp.layer, { timeout: "60 seconds" })("todos", (it) => {
  const anonymousCalls: readonly (readonly [
    string,
    (client: Client) => Effect.Effect<unknown, unknown>,
  ])[] = [
    ["list", (client) => client("todos.list", undefined)],
    ["create", (client) => client("todos.create", { title: "Buy milk" })],
    ["update", (client) => client("todos.update", { completed: true, id: 1 })],
    ["delete", (client) => client("todos.delete", { id: 1 })],
  ];

  for (const [name, call] of anonymousCalls) {
    it.effect(`rejects an anonymous ${name} as Unauthorized`, () =>
      Effect.gen(function* () {
        const client = yield* makeClient();

        const error = yield* Effect.flip(call(client));

        assert.instanceOf(error, Unauthorized);
      })
    );
  }

  it.effect("creates a todo with a trimmed title, not yet completed", () =>
    Effect.gen(function* () {
      const app = yield* TestApp;
      const cookie = yield* app.signUp;
      const client = yield* makeClient(cookie);

      const exit = yield* createRaw(cookie, "  Buy milk  ");

      assert.deepStrictEqual(exit, {
        _tag: "Success",
        value: { completed: false, title: "Buy milk" },
      });
      const [todo] = yield* client("todos.list", undefined);
      assert.deepInclude(todo, { completed: false, title: "Buy milk" });
    })
  );

  for (const [origin, contentType] of [
    ["http://localhost:4000", "text/plain"],
    ["http://localhost:4000", "application/ndjson"],
    ["https://attacker.example", "text/plain"],
    ["null", "text/plain"],
    ["", "text/plain"],
  ] as const) {
    it.effect(
      `rejects ${contentType} RPC writes from the HTTP origin ${JSON.stringify(origin)}`,
      () =>
        Effect.gen(function* () {
          const app = yield* TestApp;
          const cookie = yield* app.signUp;
          const client = yield* makeClient(cookie);

          const response = yield* Effect.promise(
            async () =>
              await app.fetch(
                new Request("http://localhost/rpc", {
                  method: "POST",
                  headers: { "content-type": contentType, cookie, origin },
                  body: `${JSON.stringify({
                    _tag: "Request",
                    id: "1",
                    headers: [["origin", "http://localhost"]],
                    tag: "todos.create",
                    payload: { title: "Unwanted todo" },
                  })}\n`,
                })
              )
          );

          yield* Effect.promise(async () => await response.text());

          assert.deepStrictEqual(yield* client("todos.list", undefined), []);
          assert.strictEqual(response.status, 403);
        })
    );
  }

  it.effect(
    "accepts the configured public origin through an internal proxy URL",
    () =>
      Effect.gen(function* () {
        const app = yield* TestApp;
        const cookie = yield* app.signUp;
        const client = yield* makeClient(cookie);

        const response = yield* Effect.promise(
          async () =>
            await app.fetch(
              new Request("http://internal.example:3000/rpc", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: {
                  "content-type": "application/ndjson",
                  cookie,
                  origin: "http://localhost",
                },
                body: `${JSON.stringify({
                  _tag: "Request",
                  id: "1",
                  headers: [["origin", "https://attacker.example"]],
                  tag: "todos.create",
                  payload: { title: "Same-origin todo" },
                })}\n`,
              })
            )
        );

        yield* Effect.promise(async () => await response.text());

        assert.strictEqual(response.status, 200);
        const [todo] = yield* client("todos.list", undefined);
        assert.deepInclude(todo, { title: "Same-origin todo" });
      })
  );

  for (const title of [
    "",
    "   ",
    "x".repeat(201),
    "😀".repeat(201),
    "nul\u0000byte",
  ]) {
    it.effect(`rejects the title ${JSON.stringify(title)}`, () =>
      Effect.gen(function* () {
        const app = yield* TestApp;
        const cookie = yield* app.signUp;
        const client = yield* makeClient(cookie);

        const exit = yield* createRaw(cookie, title);

        assert.deepStrictEqual(exit, { _tag: "Failure" });
        assert.deepStrictEqual(yield* client("todos.list", undefined), []);
      })
    );
  }

  it.effect("completes a todo, then deletes it once", () =>
    Effect.gen(function* () {
      const app = yield* TestApp;
      const client = yield* makeClient(yield* app.signUp);
      const todo = yield* client("todos.create", { title: "Buy milk" });

      assert.deepStrictEqual(
        yield* client("todos.update", { completed: true, id: todo.id }),
        { ...todo, completed: true }
      );
      yield* client("todos.delete", { id: todo.id });
      assert.deepStrictEqual(yield* client("todos.list", undefined), []);
      assert.deepStrictEqual(
        yield* Effect.flip(client("todos.delete", { id: todo.id })),
        new TodoNotFound({ id: todo.id })
      );
    })
  );

  it.effect("keeps each user's todos private", () =>
    Effect.gen(function* () {
      const app = yield* TestApp;
      const alice = yield* makeClient(yield* app.signUp);
      const bob = yield* makeClient(yield* app.signUp);
      const todo = yield* alice("todos.create", { title: "Alice's secret" });

      assert.deepStrictEqual(yield* bob("todos.list", undefined), []);
      assert.deepStrictEqual(
        yield* Effect.flip(
          bob("todos.update", { completed: true, id: todo.id })
        ),
        new TodoNotFound({ id: todo.id })
      );
      assert.deepStrictEqual(
        yield* Effect.flip(bob("todos.delete", { id: todo.id })),
        new TodoNotFound({ id: todo.id })
      );
      assert.deepStrictEqual(yield* alice("todos.list", undefined), [todo]);
    })
  );

  it.effect(
    "accepts 200 Unicode code points and trims Unicode whitespace",
    () =>
      Effect.gen(function* () {
        const app = yield* TestApp;
        const cookie = yield* app.signUp;
        const title = "😀".repeat(200);
        const exit = yield* createRaw(cookie, `\u00A0${title}\u3000`);
        assert.deepStrictEqual(exit, {
          _tag: "Success",
          value: { completed: false, title },
        });
      })
  );

  for (const id of [0, -1, 1.5, 2_147_483_648]) {
    it.effect(`rejects id ${id} before querying storage`, () =>
      Effect.gen(function* () {
        const app = yield* TestApp;
        const cookie = yield* app.signUp;
        const body = yield* rpcRaw(cookie, "todos.delete", { id });
        assert.include(body, '"_tag":"Failure"');
        assert.notInclude(body, "TodoNotFound");
        assert.notInclude(body, "ServiceUnavailable");
      })
    );
  }

  it.effect(
    "returns a typed storage failure without SQL or parameters on the wire",
    () =>
      Effect.gen(function* () {
        const app = yield* TestApp;
        const cookie = yield* app.signUp;
        const client = yield* makeClient(cookie);
        yield* Effect.acquireUseRelease(
          app.database.execute(
            sql`alter table todos rename to unavailable_todos`
          ),
          () =>
            Effect.gen(function* () {
              assert.instanceOf(
                yield* Effect.flip(client("todos.list", undefined)),
                ServiceUnavailable
              );

              const body = yield* rpcRaw(cookie, "todos.create", {
                title: "private-payload-marker",
              });

              assert.include(body, "ServiceUnavailable");
              assert.notInclude(body, "private-payload-marker");
              assert.notInclude(body, "insert into");
              assert.notInclude(body, "Drizzle");
              assert.notInclude(body, '"_tag":"Die"');
              assert.notInclude(body, '"query"');
            }),
          () =>
            app.database
              .execute(sql`alter table unavailable_todos rename to todos`)
              .pipe(Effect.orDie)
        );
      })
  );

  it.effect("sets browser security headers on public HTTP responses", () =>
    Effect.gen(function* () {
      const app = yield* TestApp;

      const response = yield* Effect.promise(
        async () => await app.fetch(new Request("http://localhost/api/health"))
      );

      assert.strictEqual(response.status, 200);
      assert.strictEqual(
        response.headers.get("x-content-type-options"),
        "nosniff"
      );
      assert.strictEqual(response.headers.get("x-frame-options"), "DENY");
    })
  );

  it.effect(
    "does not misreport an auth database outage as an anonymous session",
    () =>
      Effect.gen(function* () {
        const app = yield* TestApp;
        const cookie = yield* app.signUp;
        const client = yield* makeClient(cookie);
        yield* Effect.acquireUseRelease(
          app.database.execute(
            sql`alter table session rename to unavailable_session`
          ),
          () =>
            Effect.gen(function* () {
              assert.instanceOf(
                yield* Effect.flip(client("todos.list", undefined)),
                ServiceUnavailable
              );

              const body = yield* rpcRaw(cookie, "todos.create", {
                title: "Unsent",
              });

              assert.include(body, "ServiceUnavailable");
              assert.notInclude(body, "select");
              assert.notInclude(body, "unavailable_session");
            }),
          () =>
            app.database
              .execute(sql`alter table unavailable_session rename to session`)
              .pipe(Effect.orDie)
        );
      })
  );
});

#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { hc } from "hono/client";
import { afterAll } from "vite-plus/test";

import { createAuth } from "@my-app/auth";
import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { createApi } from "#src/index.ts";
import type { Api } from "#src/index.ts";

// Each test file has a database of its own, so files run in parallel and share no rows.
const { db, remove } = await createTestDatabase();

afterAll(remove);

const auth = createAuth({
  baseURL: "http://localhost",
  db,
  secret: "test-secret-that-is-at-least-32-characters",
});

export const api = createApi({ auth, db });

/** Sends each request to the API in this process, through its routing, validation, and serialization. */
export const createClient = (cookie?: string) =>
  hc<Api>("http://localhost", {
    fetch: api.request,
    headers: cookie === undefined ? {} : { cookie },
  });

/** Signs a new user up through Better Auth and returns a client that calls with their session cookie. */
export const signUp = async () => {
  const { headers } = await auth.api.signUpEmail({
    body: {
      email: `${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.com`,
      name: "Test User",
      password: "correct-horse-battery",
    },
    returnHeaders: true,
  });

  return createClient(
    headers
      .getSetCookie()
      .map((header) => header.split(";")[0])
      .join("; ")
  );
};

import { createRouterClient } from "@orpc/server";
import { afterAll } from "vite-plus/test";

import { createAuth } from "@my-app/auth";
import { createTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { appRouter } from "#src/index.ts";
import type { Context } from "#src/index.ts";

// Each test file has a database of its own, so files run in parallel and share no rows.
const { db, remove } = await createTestDatabase();

afterAll(remove);

const auth = createAuth({
  baseURL: "http://localhost",
  db,
  secret: "test-secret-that-is-at-least-32-characters",
});

/** Calls the procedures in this process, through their middleware and validation, as the session's user. */
export const createClient = (session: Context["session"] = null) =>
  createRouterClient(appRouter, { context: { db, session } });

/** Signs a new user up through Better Auth and returns a client that calls as them. */
export const signUp = async () => {
  const { headers } = await auth.api.signUpEmail({
    body: {
      email: `${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.com`,
      name: "Test User",
      password: "correct-horse-battery",
    },
    returnHeaders: true,
  });

  const cookie = headers
    .getSetCookie()
    .map((header) => header.split(";")[0])
    .join("; ");

  return createClient(
    await auth.api.getSession({ headers: new Headers({ cookie }) })
  );
};

# packages/api/tests/support/app.ts · 1 stack
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
import { PgClient } from "@effect/sql-pg";
import { Context, Effect, Layer, Redacted, Schema } from "effect";
import { FetchHttpClient, HttpRouter, HttpServer } from "effect/http";
import { RpcClient, RpcSerialization } from "effect/rpc";

import { Auth } from "@my-app/auth";
import { TodosRpcs } from "@my-app/contracts";
import { Database } from "@my-app/db";
import { makeTestDatabase } from "@my-app/db/testing";

import { Routes } from "#src/index.ts";

const origin = "http://localhost";

/**
 * The server's routes as a Fetch handler in this process, on a migrated database of its own, so
 * each test file runs in parallel without a port or shared rows.
 */
export class TestApp extends Context.Service<
  TestApp,
  {
    readonly database: typeof Database.Service;
    readonly fetch: (request: Request) => Promise<Response>;
    readonly signUp: Effect.Effect<string>;
  }
>()("@my-app/api/tests/TestApp") {
  static readonly layer = Layer.effect(
    TestApp,
    Effect.gen(function* () {
      const url = yield* makeTestDatabase;

      const services = yield* Layer.build(
        Layer.mergeAll(
          Database.layer.pipe(Layer.provide(PgClient.layer({ url }))),
          Auth.layer({
            baseURL: origin,
            databaseUrl: url,
            secret: Redacted.make("test-secret-that-is-at-least-32-characters"),
          })
        )
      );

      const { dispose, handler } = HttpRouter.toWebHandler(
        Routes.pipe(
          Layer.provide(Layer.succeedContext(services)),
          Layer.provide(HttpServer.layerServices)
        ),
        { disableLogger: true }
      );

      yield* Effect.addFinalizer(() => Effect.promise(dispose));

      const signUp = Effect.promise(async () => {
        const response = await handler(
          new Request(`${origin}/api/auth/sign-up/email`, {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "content-type": "application/json", origin },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              email: `${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.com`,
              name: "Test User",
              password: "correct-horse-battery",
            }),
          })
        );

        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(`Sign up failed with ${response.status}`);
        }

        const { headers } = response;

        return headers
          .getSetCookie()
          .map((header) => header.split(";")[0])
          .join("; ");
      });

      return {
        database: Context.get(services, Database),
        fetch: handler,
        signUp,
      };
    })
  );
}

export const makeClient = Effect.fnUntraced(function* (cookie?: string) {
  const app = yield* TestApp;

  const fetch = async (
    ...[input, init]: Parameters<typeof globalThis.fetch>
  ) => {
    const request = new Request(input, init);

    if (cookie !== undefined) {
      request.headers.set("cookie", cookie);
    }

    return await app.fetch(request);
  };

  return yield* RpcClient.make(TodosRpcs, { flatten: true }).pipe(
    Effect.provide(
      RpcClient.layerProtocolHttp({ url: `${origin}/rpc` }).pipe(
        Layer.provide([RpcSerialization.layerNdjson, FetchHttpClient.layer]),
        Layer.provide(Layer.succeed(FetchHttpClient.Fetch, fetch))
      )
    )
  );
});

const CreateExit = Schema.fromJsonString(
  Schema.Struct({
    exit: Schema.Union([
      Schema.Struct({
        _tag: Schema.Literal("Success"),
        value: Schema.Struct({
          completed: Schema.Boolean,
          title: Schema.String,
        }),
      }),
      Schema.Struct({ _tag: Schema.Literal("Failure") }),
    ]),
  })
);

/**
 * Posts raw NDJSON past client validation, so tests observe the server's actual wire response.
 */
export const rpcRaw = Effect.fnUntraced(function* (
  cookie: string,
  tag: string,
  payload: Record<string, string | number | boolean>
) {
  const app = yield* TestApp;

  const response = yield* Effect.promise(
    async () =>
      await app.fetch(
        new Request(`${origin}/rpc`, {
          body: `${JSON.stringify({ _tag: "Request", headers: [], id: "1", payload, tag })}\n`,
          headers: { "content-type": "application/ndjson", cookie },
          method: "POST",
        })
      )
  );

  return yield* Effect.promise(async () => await response.text());
});

export const createRaw = Effect.fnUntraced(function* (
  cookie: string,
  title: string
) {
  const body = yield* rpcRaw(cookie, "todos.create", { title });
  const { exit } = yield* Schema.decodeUnknownEffect(CreateExit)(body.trim());

  return exit;
});

// Word characters read the same in SQL and in every driver's URL parsing, and 49 of them with the
// suffix stay within PostgreSQL's 63 bytes and MySQL's 64 characters.
export const testDatabaseName = (configured: string) =>
  `${configured.replaceAll(/\W/gu, "_").slice(0, 49)}_test_${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;

#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mongodb-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mongodb-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mongodb-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mongodb-expo-docker-e2e
import { createDb } from "#src/index.ts";
import { migrateDatabase } from "#src/migrate.ts";

/**
 * A database of its own beside `DATABASE_URL`'s, with its indexes built and dropped by `remove`. The random
 * name lets parallel test files, runners, and checkouts share one MongoDB server.
 */
export const createTestDatabase = async () => {
  const { DATABASE_URL } = process.env;

  if (DATABASE_URL === undefined) {
    throw new Error("DATABASE_URL is not set");
  }

  const url = new URL(DATABASE_URL);
  url.pathname = `${url.pathname}_test_${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;

  const db = createDb(url.href);

  const remove = async () => {
    try {
      await db.connection.dropDatabase();
    } finally {
      await db.connection.close();
    }
  };

  try {
    await migrateDatabase(db);
  } catch (error) {
    await remove();
    throw error;
  }

  return { db, remove, url: url.href };
};

#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mysql-void-e2e
import { createDb } from "#src/index.ts";
import { migrateDatabase } from "#src/migrate.ts";
import { testDatabaseName } from "#src/test-database-name.ts";

const runAsAdmin = async (adminUrl: URL, statement: string) => {
  const admin = createDb(adminUrl.href);

  try {
    await admin.$client.promise().query(statement);
  } finally {
    await admin.$client.promise().end();
  }
};

/**
 * A migrated database of its own beside `DATABASE_URL`, dropped by `remove`. The random name lets
 * parallel test files, runners, and checkouts share one MySQL server.
 */
export const createTestDatabase = async () => {
  const { DATABASE_URL } = process.env;

  if (DATABASE_URL === undefined) {
    throw new Error("DATABASE_URL is not set");
  }

  const adminUrl = new URL(DATABASE_URL);
  const url = new URL(adminUrl);
  const name = testDatabaseName(adminUrl.pathname.slice(1));
  url.pathname = `/${name}`;

  await runAsAdmin(adminUrl, `create database \`${name}\``);
  const db = createDb(url.href);

  const remove = async () => {
    try {
      await db.$client.promise().end();
    } finally {
      await runAsAdmin(adminUrl, `drop database \`${name}\``);
    }
  };

  try {
    await migrateDatabase(db);
  } catch (error) {
    await remove();
    throw error;
  }

  return { db, remove, url: url.href };
};

import { createDb } from "#src/index.ts";
import { migrateDatabase } from "#src/migrate.ts";
import { testDatabaseName } from "#src/test-database-name.ts";

const runAsAdmin = async (adminUrl: URL, statement: string) => {
  const admin = createDb(adminUrl.href);

  try {
    await admin.$executeRawUnsafe(statement);
  } finally {
    await admin.$disconnect();
  }
};

/**
 * A migrated database of its own beside `DATABASE_URL`, dropped by `remove`. The random name lets
 * parallel test files, runners, and checkouts share one MySQL server.
 */
export const createTestDatabase = async () => {
  const { DATABASE_URL } = process.env;

  if (DATABASE_URL === undefined) {
    throw new Error("DATABASE_URL is not set");
  }

  const adminUrl = new URL(DATABASE_URL);
  const url = new URL(adminUrl);
  const name = testDatabaseName(adminUrl.pathname.slice(1));
  url.pathname = `/${name}`;

  await runAsAdmin(adminUrl, `create database \`${name}\``);
  const db = createDb(url.href);

  const remove = async () => {
    try {
      await db.$disconnect();
    } finally {
      await runAsAdmin(adminUrl, `drop database \`${name}\``);
    }
  };

  try {
    await migrateDatabase(db);
  } catch (error) {
    await remove();
    throw error;
  }

  return { db, remove, url: url.href };
};

import { createDb } from "#src/index.ts";
import { migrateDatabase } from "#src/migrate.ts";
import { testDatabaseName } from "#src/test-database-name.ts";

const runAsAdmin = async (adminUrl: URL, statement: string) => {
  const admin = createDb(adminUrl.href);

  try {
    await admin.$client.unsafe(statement);
  } finally {
    await admin.$client.end();
  }
};

/**
 * A migrated database of its own beside `DATABASE_URL`, dropped by `remove`. The random name lets
 * parallel test files, runners, and checkouts share one PostgreSQL server.
 */
export const createTestDatabase = async () => {
  const { DATABASE_URL } = process.env;

  if (DATABASE_URL === undefined) {
    throw new Error("DATABASE_URL is not set");
  }

  const adminUrl = new URL(DATABASE_URL);
  const url = new URL(adminUrl);
  const name = testDatabaseName(adminUrl.pathname.slice(1));
  url.pathname = `/${name}`;

  await runAsAdmin(adminUrl, `create database "${name}"`);
  const db = createDb(url.href);

  const remove = async () => {
    try {
      await db.$client.end();
    } finally {
      await runAsAdmin(adminUrl, `drop database "${name}" with (force)`);
    }
  };

  try {
    await migrateDatabase(db);
  } catch (error) {
    await remove();
    throw error;
  }

  return { db, remove, url: url.href };
};

#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-expo-docker-e2e
import { mkdtemp, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";

import { createDb } from "#src/index.ts";
import { migrateDatabase } from "#src/migrate.ts";

/** A migrated database of its own in a fresh temporary directory, deleted by `remove`. */
export const createTestDatabase = async () => {
  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), "my-app-test-"));
  const url = path.join(dir, "test.db");
  const db = createDb(url);
  migrateDatabase(db);

  return {
    db,
    remove: async () => {
      db.$client.close();
      await rm(dir, { force: true, maxRetries: 10, recursive: true });
    },
    url,
  };
};

# packages/db/src/testing.ts · 1 stack
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
import { PgClient } from "@effect/sql-pg";
import { Config, Effect, Layer, Redacted } from "effect";
import { SqlClient } from "effect/sql";

import { Database } from "#src/index.ts";
import { migrateDatabase } from "#src/migrate.ts";
import { testDatabaseName } from "#src/test-database-name.ts";

const runAsAdmin = Effect.fn("TestDatabase.runAsAdmin")(
  (adminUrl: URL, statement: string) =>
    SqlClient.SqlClient.pipe(
      Effect.flatMap((sql) => sql.unsafe(statement)),
      Effect.provide(PgClient.layer({ url: Redacted.make(adminUrl.href) }))
    )
);

export const makeTestDatabase = Effect.gen(function* () {
  const adminUrl = new URL(
    Redacted.value(yield* Config.Redacted("DATABASE_URL"))
  );

  const name = testDatabaseName(adminUrl.pathname.slice(1));
  const url = new URL(adminUrl);
  url.pathname = `/${name}`;

  yield* Effect.acquireRelease(
    runAsAdmin(adminUrl, `create database "${name}"`),
    () =>
      Effect.orDie(runAsAdmin(adminUrl, `drop database "${name}" with (force)`))
  );
  yield* migrateDatabase.pipe(
    Effect.provide(
      Database.layer.pipe(
        Layer.provide(PgClient.layer({ url: Redacted.make(url.href) }))
      )
    )
  );

  return Redacted.make(url.href);
});
